Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in autoimmunity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 94 and the invariant I(A,B) = 177 mod 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 94 + O(1/n^177). Summing over all modes and applying the autoimmunity convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
